       886 +                                                             
       887 +class _SparkPainter extends CustomPainter {                  
       888 +  _SparkPainter({                                            
       889 +    required this.values,                                    
       890 +    required this.accent,                                    
       891 +    required this.progress,                                  
       892 +  });                                                        
       893 +  final List<double> values;                                 
       894 +  final Color accent;                                        
       895 +  final double progress;                                     
       896 +                                                             
       897 +  @override                                                  
       898 +  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {                     
       899 +    if (values.length < 2) return;                           
       900 +                                                             
       901 +    // Skip leading zeros (no data for those weeks). Use the 
           +trailing run.                                                
       902 +    final firstNonZero = values.indexWhere((v) => v > 0);    
       903 +    final plot = firstNonZero < 0                            
       904 +        ? values                                             
       905 +        : values.sublist(firstNonZero);                      
       906 +    if (plot.length < 2) return;                             
       907 +                                                             
       908 +    final maxV = plot.reduce(math.max);                      
       909 +    final minV = plot.where((v) => v > 0).fold<double>(      
       910 +          maxV,                                              
       911 +          (a, b) => b < a ? b : a,                           
       912 +        );                                                   
       913 +    final span = (maxV - minV).abs() < 1e-6 ? 1.0 : (maxV - m
           +inV);                                                        
       914 +                                                             
       915 +    final points = <Offset>[];                               
       916 +    for (var i = 0; i < plot.length; i++) {                  
       917 +      final x = (i / (plot.length - 1)) * size.width;        
       918 +      final y = size.height - ((plot[i] - minV) / span) * (si
           +ze.height - 6) - 3;                                          
       919 +      points.add(Offset(x, y));                              
       920 +    }                                                        
       921 +                                                             
       922 +    // Area under curve.                                     
       923 +    final areaPath = Path()..moveTo(points.first.dx, size.hei
           +ght);                                                        
       924 +    for (final p in points) {                                
       925 +      areaPath.lineTo(p.dx, p.dy);                           
       926 +    }                                                        
       927 +    areaPath                                                 
       928 +      ..lineTo(points.last.dx, size.height)                  
       929 +      ..close();                                             
       930 +    final areaPaint = Paint()                                
       931 +      ..shader = LinearGradient(                             
       932 +        begin: Alignment.topCenter,                          
       933 +        end: Alignment.bottomCenter,                         
       934 +        colors: [                                            
       935 +          accent.withValues(alpha: 0.4 * progress),          
       936 +          accent.withValues(alpha: 0),                       
       937 +        ],                                                   
       938 +      ).createShader(Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, size.width, size.hei
           +ght));                                                       
       939 +    canvas.drawPath(areaPath, areaPaint);                    
       940 +                                                             
       941 +    // Line, animated via trim.                              
       942 +    final linePath = Path()..moveTo(points.first.dx, points.f
           +irst.dy);                                                    
       943 +    for (var i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {                
       944 +      linePath.lineTo(points[i].dx, points[i].dy);           
       945 +    }                                                        
       946 +    final metric = linePath.computeMetrics().first;          
       947 +    final drawn = metric.extractPath(0, metric.length * progr
           +ess);                                                        
       948 +    final linePaint = Paint()                                
       949 +      ..color = accent                                       
       950 +      ..strokeWidth = 2                                      
       951 +      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke                         
       952 +      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round                          
       953 +      ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round;                       
       954 +    canvas.drawPath(drawn, linePaint);                       
       955 +                                                             
       956 +    // Dots — each appears once the line passes its position.
       957 +    final dotPaint = Paint()..color = Colors.white;          
       958 +    for (var i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {                
       959 +      final showAt = i / (points.length - 1);                
       960 +      if (progress < showAt) continue;                       
       961 +      final localT = ((progress - showAt) / 0.15).clamp(0.0, 
           +1.0);                                                        
       962 +      canvas.drawCircle(points[i], 3 * localT, dotPaint);    
       963 +    }                                                        
       964 +  }                                                          
       965 +                                                             
       966 +  @override                                                  
       967 +  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _SparkPainter old) {          
       968 +    return old.progress != progress ||                       
       969 +        old.accent != accent ||                              
       970 +        old.values != values;                                
       971 +  }                                                          
       972 +}

● Recap completamente rediseñado. Resumen:

  Data layer — MonthlyRecap ganó 6 campos nuevos (prevSessionsCount,
  bestWeekSessions, newPRsCount, weeklyVolumeKg, volumeByMuscle, topLift) y una
  sub-entity RecapTopLift. _computeRecap ahora bucketea sesiones por semana del
  mes (volumen + best week), cuenta cuántos ejercicios batieron e1RM histórico
  (newPRsCount), elige el top lift por mayor e1RM individual con su delta vs mes
   anterior y una sparkline de 7 semanas, y agrupa volumen en los 6 buckets del
  diseño (Chest/Back/Legs/Shoulders/Arms/Core).

  Screen — Stories container (monthly_recap_screen.dart reescrito) —
  AnimationController con duración por slide, auto-advance al completar, tap
  zona izquierda 35% retrocede / derecha 65% avanza, long-press pausa con
  onLongPressStart/onLongPressEnd. Progress bars top que se llenan al ritmo del
  controller. Close button glass top-right. PageView eliminado.

  Backdrop nuevo (recap_backdrop.dart) — base #0E0B07 + dos RadialGradient
  estáticos (warm top-right, deep bottom-left) usando
  context.colors.accent/accentDeep + grain texture vía CustomPainter. El aurora
  animado fue eliminado.

  7 slides nuevos (recap_slides.dart reescrito):
  - CoverSlide: eyebrow mes/año + "Recap" gradient + subtitle + "TAP TO BEGIN"
  flotando.
  - SessionsSlide: count-up gigante (150px) + delta pill + footer con avg
  semanal.
  - VolumeSlide: count-up con sufijo "kg" + 4-5 barras semanales con gradient
  accent y growth animation staggered.
  - CalendarSlide: día labels + grid mes-completo con celdas de gradient para
  días entrenados, staggered entry con overshoot, footer con "Best week".
  - TopLiftSlide: nombre + count-up + delta pill + card con sparkline animada
  (CustomPainter que traza la línea progresivamente, área con gradient, dots con
   stagger).
  - MuscleBalanceSlide: top músculo gradient + 6 barras ordenadas por %, top con
   glow.
  - OutroSlide: trio de stat cards (sessions/hours/new PRs accent) + headline +
  footer + CTA "Back to training".

  Helpers compartidos: _GradientText (ShaderMask blanco→accentLight→accent),
  _CountUp (TweenAnimationBuilder con easeOutCubic + thousands separator),
  _Eyebrow, _DeltaPill (accent-tinted con tabular nums).

  Banner movido a Home — _RecapHomeBanner con gradient dark warm (#29211A →
